diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing_day_two.md b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing_day_two.md
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..8512f08385
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing_day_two.md
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+
+## Day-two proof with production accounts
+
+Run this in a fresh `init --web` starter after installing its dependencies.
+Save the block as scripts/day2-accounts.mjs and run `node scripts/day2-accounts.mjs`.
+It uses the starter's complete production recipe (teams and memberships),
+compiles and builds that UI, then signs in through its production account form.
+The production entry recipe is maintained on `guide production`.
+Adapt the assertions to your own business model when replacing this example.
+
+The proof keeps one live serve process and one browser page through v1 → v2 →
+rollback → forward. It tests real password accounts, a rendered durable write,
+existing-row defaults, a new membership, authorization refusals, and retained
+rows at every transition. Saved sources and deployments belong to its private
+scratch directory. It changes the fresh starter's working schema and UI to the
+production recipe; run it in a copy when preserving an existing application.
+
+<!-- DAY TWO ACCOUNTS BEGIN -->
+```js
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { copyFile, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { signup } from "snapback4/auth";
+import { withBrowser, command, cli, deploy, rollback, startServe, startStatic,
+  fillRN, bounded, renderedText, waitForFresh, waitForServer, cleanup } from "snapback4/proof";
+
+await withBrowser({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME }, async context => {
+  // Browser dependency checks happen before building or deploying anything.
+  for (const [from, to] of [["schema.q", "snapback/schema.q"], ["App.tsx", "src/App.tsx"], ["client.ts", "src/client.ts"]]) {
+    await copyFile(`recipes/production/${from}`, to);
+  }
+  for (const args of [["check"]]) { const result = await cli(args); assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.stderr); }
+  for (const script of ["typecheck", "build"]) {
+    const result = await command("npm", ["run", script]); assert.equal(result.code, 0, result.stderr);
+  }
+  const work = await mkdtemp(join(process.cwd(), ".day2-accounts-"));
+  let serve, web;
+  try {
+    const v1 = join(work, "v1"), v2 = join(work, "v2"), directory = join(work, "deployment");
+    await mkdir(v1); await mkdir(v2);
+    const base = await readFile("recipes/production/schema.q", "utf8");
+    const evolved = base.replace("table members:\n", "table members:\n  note: text <=40 default 'evolved'\n");
+    assert.notEqual(evolved, base);
+    await writeFile(join(v1, "schema.q"), base); await writeFile(join(v2, "schema.q"), evolved);
+    await deploy(directory, v1);
+    serve = await startServe(directory); web = await startStatic("dist");
+    const password = "Proof-password-42!", email = `admin-${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.test`;
+    const admin = await signup(serve.url, email, password);
+    const member = await signup(serve.url, `member-${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.test`, password);
+    const outsider = await signup(serve.url, `outsider-${crypto.randomUUID()}@example.test`, password);
+    for (const account of [admin, member, outsider]) assert.equal(account.ok, true, JSON.stringify(account));
+    const page = await context.newPage();
+    await page.goto(`${web.url}/?api=${encodeURIComponent(serve.url)}`, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
+    await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Email", { exact: true }), email);
+    await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Password", { exact: true }), password);
+    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in", exact: true }).click();
+    await waitForFresh(renderedText(page, "Teams and roles"), null);
+    assert.equal(await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^Dev:/ }).count(), 0, "serve offers accounts only");
+    const title = `Team ${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
+    await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Team name", { exact: true }), title);
+    const create = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create team", exact: true });
+    await create.evaluate((button, title) => window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm("createTeam", { title }, button), title);
+    await create.click();
+    const receipt = await bounded(page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.formResult()), 30000, "team receipt");
+    assert.equal(receipt.state, "sent", JSON.stringify(receipt));
+    const teamId = receipt.result.id;
+    async function call(account, door, name, args) {
+      const response = await fetch(`${serve.url}/${door}/${name}`, { method: "POST",
+        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", authorization: `Bearer ${account.session.token}` },
+        body: JSON.stringify({ ...(door === "m" ? { id: crypto.randomUUID() } : {}), args }) });
+      assert.equal(response.status, 200); return response.json();
+    }
+    async function business(label, evolved, joined) {
+      const own = await waitForServer(() => call(admin, "q", "myRole", { teamId }), read => read.complete === true, { label });
+      assert.equal(own.data.principal, admin.session.principal); assert.equal(own.data.role, "admin");
+      assert.equal(own.data.note, evolved ? "evolved" : undefined, label);
+      const roster = await call(admin, "q", "roster", { teamId, c: null });
+      assert.equal(roster.complete, true); assert.equal(roster.data.length, joined ? 2 : 1, label);
+      assert.ok(roster.data.some(row => row.principal === admin.session.principal));
+      if (joined) assert.ok(roster.data.some(row => row.principal === member.session.principal));
+      assert.equal((await call(outsider, "q", "myRole", { teamId })).denied.code, "NOT_MEMBER", label);
+      const refused = await call(outsider, "m", "invite", { teamId, principal: outsider.session.principal, role: "admin" });
+      assert.equal(refused.state, "failed"); assert.equal(refused.why.code, "NOT_ADMIN", label);
+      await waitForFresh(renderedText(page, new RegExp(teamId), { within: page.locator("main") }), null);
+    }
+    await business("v1", false, false);
+    await deploy(directory, v2);
+    const invited = await call(admin, "m", "invite", { teamId, principal: member.session.principal, role: "member" });
+    assert.equal(invited.state, "sent", JSON.stringify(invited));
+    await business("v2", true, true);
+    await rollback(directory, 1); await business("rollback-v1", false, true);
+    await rollback(directory, "latest"); await business("forward-v2", true, true);
+    console.log("day-two accounts passed: v1, hot v2, rollback, forward; business assertions at every step");
+  } finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => serve?.stop(), () => rm(work, { recursive: true, force: true })); }
+});
+```
+<!-- DAY TWO ACCOUNTS END -->
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/wire.md b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/wire.md
index 42262d3c90..04976ab2a1 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/wire.md
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/wire.md
@@ -2,16 +2,100 @@
 
 Every call carries a principal; public visitors use guest sessions; `serve` accepts bearers only. Plain JSON over HTTP works with curl, scripts and non-JS clients.
 
-```sh
-U=http://127.0.0.1:4400
-curl -s -X POST $U/q/mine -H 'x-snapback-persona: alice' -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"args":{}}'
-curl -s -X POST $U/m/add -H 'x-snapback-persona: alice' -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"args":{"body":"hi"}}'
+## First raw HTTP proof
+
+| Send | Inspect the JSON body |
+|---|---|
+| `POST /q/name {"args":{…}}` | `{data, complete}` or `{denied:{…}}` |
+| `POST /m/name {"id":"one-intent","args":{…}}` | `{state:"sent", result}` or `{state:"failed", why}` |
+| `POST /seed [{"table":"notes","row":{…}}]` | Bare array; `{seeded}` or `{denied}` |
+| `POST /dev/reset {"seed":[{"table":"notes","row":{…}}]}` | Wrapper; `{reset:true, seeded, …}` or `{denied}` |
+
+Dev uses `x-snapback-persona: alice`. Serve uses `Authorization: Bearer <token>`
+from a real account or service seat. Send one credential with `Content-Type:
+application/json`. Authentication can refuse HTTP 401 before either operation;
+HTTP 200 still requires inspecting `denied`, `state`, and `why`.
+Relative times such as `"now+1h"` work in typed seed/fixture time fields and
+fixture case arguments. `/m` takes Unix milliseconds; it never interprets
+relative time strings. Query data and mutation results have different envelopes.
+
+Save this schema as snapback/schema.q in a scratch project with snapback4
+installed. Save the JavaScript as scripts/wire-proof.mjs and run it with Node.
+Qualification executes these exact blocks through the packed CLI and generates
+the trace below. Minted identifiers, timestamps and observed durations alone
+are normalized; refusal codes, request bodies and envelope fields are retained.
+
+<!-- WIRE FIRST SCHEMA BEGIN -->
+```quarry
+use personas alice, bob
+table notes:
+  title: text <=80
+  at: time
+  public 'literal wire proof'
+  insert <- allow
+  online only
+query list(c: cursor ?):
+  require viewer = 'dev:alice' else READ_REFUSED
+  return notes first 10 by .id after c
+mutation add(title: text <=80, at: time):
+  require title != 'refuse' else WRITE_REFUSED
+  return insert notes { title, at }
 ```
+<!-- WIRE FIRST SCHEMA END -->
+
+<!-- WIRE FIRST SCRIPT BEGIN -->
+```js
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { startDev, cleanup } from "snapback4/proof";
+const dev = await startDev();
+const trace = [];
+async function post(path, body, persona = "alice") {
+  const headers = { "content-type": "application/json", "x-snapback-persona": persona };
+  const response = await fetch(dev.url + path, { method: "POST", headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
+  const result = await response.json();
+  trace.push({ request: { method: "POST", path, headers, body }, response: { status: response.status, body: result } });
+  return result;
+}
+try {
+  const seed = [{ table: "notes", row: { id: "seed", title: "Seed", at: "now+1h" } }];
+  assert.equal((await post("/seed", seed)).seeded, 1);
+  const reset = await post("/dev/reset", { seed });
+  assert.equal(reset.reset, true); assert.notEqual(reset.storeId, reset.previousStoreId);
+  const at = await dev.now();
+  const sent = await post("/m/add", { id: "proof-write", args: { title: "Proof", at } });
+  assert.equal(sent.state, "sent"); assert.equal(sent.id, "proof-write"); assert.ok(sent.result.id);
+  const failed = await post("/m/add", { id: "refused-write", args: { title: "refuse", at } });
+  assert.equal(failed.state, "failed"); assert.equal(failed.why.code, "WRITE_REFUSED");
+  const read = await post("/q/list", { args: { c: null } });
+  assert.equal(read.complete, true); assert.equal(read.data.length, 2);
+  assert.equal((await post("/q/list", { args: { c: null } }, "bob")).denied.code, "READ_REFUSED");
+  assert.equal((await post("/m/add", { args: { title: "Relative", at: "now+1h" } })).why.code, "E_INPUT");
+  const normalize = (value, key = "") => {
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(item => normalize(item));
+    if (value && typeof value === "object") return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([k,v]) => [k, normalize(v,k)]));
+    if (["store_id", "storeId", "previousStoreId"].includes(key)) return "<store-id>";
+    if (key === "id" && !["seed", "proof-write", "refused-write"].includes(value)) return "<row-id>";
+    if (["ms", "skewMs"].includes(key)) return "<observed-ms>";
+    return typeof value === "number" && value > 1_000_000_000_000 ? "<unix-ms>" : value;
+  };
+  console.log(JSON.stringify(normalize(trace), null, 2));
+} finally { await cleanup(() => dev.stop()); }
+```
+<!-- WIRE FIRST SCRIPT END -->
 
-`snapback4 dev` serves on that address (choose another with `--port N`, or
-`--port 0` for a free one; the first stdout line is the URL). A taken port
-refuses the start at once with `E_ADDRINUSE`, naming the holder's pid. The JS client (`snapback4/client`) stays the
-paved surface for apps: it adds sync, offline writes and live queries.
+<!-- WIRE FIRST TRACE BEGIN -->
+```json
+[
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/seed","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":[{"table":"notes","row":{"id":"seed","title":"Seed","at":"now+1h"}}]},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"seeded":1,"generation":1}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/dev/reset","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":{"seed":[{"table":"notes","row":{"id":"seed","title":"Seed","at":"now+1h"}}]}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"assetsRemoved":0,"clockOffsetMs":0,"generation":1,"head":3,"ms":"<observed-ms>","now":"<unix-ms>","previousStoreId":"<store-id>","reset":true,"seeded":1,"skewMs":"<observed-ms>","storeId":"<store-id>"}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/m/add","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"Proof","at":"<unix-ms>"}}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"generation":1,"head":4,"id":"proof-write","preserved":["notes"],"result":{"at":"<unix-ms>","id":"<row-id>","title":"Proof"},"seq":4,"state":"sent","store_id":"<store-id>"}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/m/add","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":{"id":"refused-write","args":{"title":"refuse","at":"<unix-ms>"}}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"generation":1,"head":4,"id":"refused-write","preserved":[],"state":"failed","store_id":"<store-id>","why":{"code":"WRITE_REFUSED","family":"rule","guide":"programs","message":"the program refused with WRITE_REFUSED","rule":"WRITE_REFUSED","site":"schema.q:12:3"}}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/q/list","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":{"args":{"c":null}}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"scopes":{},"generation":1,"complete":true,"cut":null,"data":[{"at":"<unix-ms>","id":"<row-id>","title":"Proof"},{"at":"<unix-ms>","id":"seed","title":"Seed"}],"next":null,"nextExpiry":null,"seq":4,"store_id":"<store-id>","tables":["notes"],"viewer":"dev:alice","meter":{"cut":null,"examined":2,"heapBytes":635,"indexEntries":0,"outputBytes":198,"probes":0,"rowsWritten":0,"steps":7}}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/q/list","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"bob"},"body":{"args":{"c":null}}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"complete":false,"denied":{"code":"READ_REFUSED","family":"rule","guide":"programs","message":"the program refused with READ_REFUSED","rule":"READ_REFUSED","site":"schema.q:9:3"},"generation":1,"head":4,"seq":4,"store_id":"<store-id>"}}},
+{"request":{"method":"POST","path":"/m/add","headers":{"content-type":"application/json","x-snapback-persona":"alice"},"body":{"args":{"title":"Relative","at":"now+1h"}}},"response":{"status":200,"body":{"generation":1,"head":4,"state":"failed","store_id":"<store-id>","why":{"code":"E_INPUT","family":"input","guide":"types","message":"argument at: expected a time in milliseconds, got a string"}}}}
+]
+```
+<!-- WIRE FIRST TRACE END -->
 
 ## Identity
 
@@ -186,7 +270,7 @@ Dev only, same machine; `serve` refuses each one.
   (signed-up users, guests). The adopted schema, personas and service seats
   (`snapback4 token`, the dev worker's) stay. The store takes a new identity:
   a local client re-acquires the new store by itself, an online client halts
-  with `E_STORE_REPLACED` (`createApp` reopens it). The head keeps counting.
+  with `E_DEV_RESET: reopen the client` (`createApp` reopens it). The head keeps counting.
   The body is empty, a `/seed` array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}`
   (either key optional): seed rows are written as `/seed` writes them, in the
   same transaction (a refused row resets nothing), and `"clock": "reset"`

diff --git a/snapback4/scripts/publication-proof-kit.mjs b/snapback4/scripts/publication-proof-kit.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..9580e6e099
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/scripts/publication-proof-kit.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,90 @@
+// Packed guide recipes and pre-wave controls. Every example executes unchanged.
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {mkdirSync,readFileSync,writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';
+import {join,resolve} from 'node:path';
+import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
+import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
+import {runToolchainCommand} from './publication-starters.mjs';
+import {chromiumExecutable} from '../witness/lib.mjs';
+const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('../',import.meta.url));
+export async function proveProofKit(client,platform,directory) {
+  const cwd=join(resolve(directory),'proof-kit');mkdirSync(cwd,{recursive:true});
+  const env={...process.env,CI:'1',CHROME:process.env.CHROME??chromiumExecutable(),npm_config_audit:'false',npm_config_fund:'false'};
+  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;delete env.PORT_LO;delete env.PORT_HI;
+  const run=(command,args)=>runToolchainCommand(command,args,{cwd,env,timeout:300000});
+  const capture=args=>{
+    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[join(cwd,'node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js'),...args],{cwd,env,encoding:'utf8',timeout:30000});
+    assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stdout+result.stderr);return result.stdout;
+  };
+  writeFileSync(join(cwd,'package.json'),'{"private":true,"type":"module"}');
+  await run('npm',['install','--no-save','--ignore-scripts','--no-audit','--no-fund',resolve(client),resolve(platform)]);
+  const wrapper=join(cwd,'node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js');
+  await run(process.execPath,[wrapper,'init','--web']);
+  const pkg=JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd,'package.json')));
+  pkg.dependencies.snapback4=`file:${resolve(client)}`;
+  pkg.optionalDependencies={[`snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`]:`file:${resolve(platform)}`};
+  writeFileSync(join(cwd,'package.json'),JSON.stringify(pkg,null,2));
+  await run('npm',['install','--prefer-offline','--no-audit','--no-fund']);
+  env.PATH=join(cwd,'node_modules/.bin')+':'+env.PATH;
+  const guide=capture(['guide','testing','--full']);writeFileSync(join(cwd,'testing-guide.md'),guide);
+  const recipe=guide.match(/<!-- DAY TWO ACCOUNTS BEGIN -->\n```js\n([\s\S]*?)\n```\n<!-- DAY TWO ACCOUNTS END -->/)?.[1];
+  assert.ok(recipe,'packed guide has the complete day-two proof');
+  writeFileSync(join(cwd,'day2-accounts.mjs'),recipe);
+  await run(process.execPath,['day2-accounts.mjs']);
+  writeFileSync(join(cwd,'clock-controls.mjs'),clockControls);
+  await run(process.execPath,['clock-controls.mjs']);
+  const testEnv={...env,SNAPBACK4_BIN:join(cwd,`node_modules/snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}/snapback4`),SNAPBACK4_PROOF_PACKAGE:join(cwd,'node_modules/snapback4')};
+  await runToolchainCommand(process.execPath,['--test',join(root,'packages/snapback4/test/wire-first.test.mjs')],{cwd,env:testEnv,timeout:60000});
+  return {status:'passed',dayTwo:'production accounts, UI sign-in, v1/hot v2/rollback/forward business assertions',wire:'literal first section, actual packed CLI trace',clock:'30-day coalesced jump, each-boundary cap, memory replay command, page sampling',aliases:'array index path through packed test'};
+}
+const clockControls=String.raw`
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import {mkdtemp,mkdir,writeFile,rm} from 'node:fs/promises';
+import {tmpdir} from 'node:os';
+import {join} from 'node:path';
+import {startDev,startStatic,withBrowser,waitForClock,renderedText,waitForFresh,cli,command,cleanup} from 'snapback4/proof';
+const cwd=await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(),'kit4-packed-clock-'));
+let dev,web,uiDev;
+try {
+  await mkdir(join(cwd,'snapback'));await mkdir(join(cwd,'fixtures'));
+  const source="use personas alice\ntable counters:\n  n: int\n  cap 1\n  public 'proof'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\nquery counts():\n  return counters first 1 by .id\nmutation bump(counterId: counters):\n  row = counters[counterId] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  update counters[row] {n:row.n+1}\n  return null\njob tick():\n  chunk row in counters first 1 by byId:\n    update counters[row.id] {n:row.n+1}\njob secondTick():\n  chunk row in counters first 1 by byId:\n    update counters[row.id] {n:row.n+1}\nschedule first every 5m: tick\nschedule second every 5m: secondTick\n";
+  await writeFile(join(cwd,'snapback/schema.q'),source);
+  await writeFile(join(cwd,'fixtures/counters.jsonl'),'{"id":"counter","n":0}\n');
+  await writeFile(join(cwd,'fixtures/test.cases.jsonl'),[
+    {as:'alice',op:'counts',args:{},save:'rows',count:1},
+    {as:'alice',op:'bump',args:{counterId:'$rows.0.id'}},
+    {as:'alice',op:'counts',args:{},match:[{n:1}]}].map(JSON.stringify).join('\n'));
+  const tested=await cli(['test'],{cwd});assert.equal(tested.code,0,tested.stdout+tested.stderr);
+  dev=await startDev({cwd,args:['--no-watch']});
+  const post=async(path,body)=>(await fetch(dev.url+path,{method:'POST',headers:{'x-snapback-persona':'alice'},body:JSON.stringify(body)})).json();
+  await post('/seed',[{table:'counters',row:{id:'counter',n:0}}]);
+  const refused=await post('/dev/clock/advance',{duration:'30d',eachBoundary:true});
+  assert.equal(refused.denied.code,'E_CLOCK_INPUT');assert.match(refused.denied.message,/--each-boundary.*10000.*omit --each-boundary/);
+  assert.equal((await (await fetch(dev.url+'/dev/status')).json()).clockOffsetMs,0);
+  const advanced=await post('/dev/clock/advance',{duration:'30d'});
+  assert.equal(advanced.offsetMs,30*86400000);assert.equal(advanced.schedules.length,2);
+  for(const schedule of advanced.schedules) {assert.equal(schedule.firedWindows.length,1);assert.ok(schedule.skippedPeriods>=8639&&schedule.skippedPeriods<=8640);assert.equal(schedule.skippedBoundariesOmitted,schedule.skippedPeriods-32);}
+  const count=async()=> (await post('/q/counts',{args:{}})).data[0].n;
+  assert.equal(await count(),2);
+  const replay=advanced.schedules[0].skippedBoundaries[0].command;
+  assert.ok(replay.includes('--port '+new URL(dev.url).port),replay);
+  const replayed=await command('sh',['-c',replay],{cwd});assert.equal(replayed.code,0,replayed.stderr);assert.equal(await count(),3);
+  await dev.stop();dev=undefined;
+  // The built production starter uses its own real client and witness clock.
+  uiDev=await startDev();web=await startStatic('dist');
+  await withBrowser({executablePath:process.env.CHROME},async context=>{
+    const page=await context.newPage();await page.goto(web.url+'/?api='+encodeURIComponent(uiDev.url));
+    await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Continue as a guest',exact:true}).click();
+    await waitForFresh(renderedText(page,'Teams and roles'),null);
+    const response=await fetch(uiDev.url+'/dev/clock/advance',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({duration:'2m'})});
+    const advanced=await response.json();assert.ok(!advanced.denied,JSON.stringify(advanced));
+    assert.ok(await waitForClock(page,advanced.now)>=advanced.now);
+  });
+  console.log('packed clock and alias controls passed');
+} finally {await cleanup(()=>web?.stop(),()=>uiDev?.stop(),()=>dev?.stop(),()=>rm(cwd,{recursive:true,force:true}));}
+`;
+if(process.argv[1]&&resolve(process.argv[1])===fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
+  const [client,platform,out]=process.argv.slice(2);
+  if(!client||!platform||!out) throw Error('usage: publication-proof-kit.mjs client.tgz platform.tgz output-directory');
+  console.log(JSON.stringify(await proveProofKit(client,platform,out),null,2));
+}

+## Proof API (generated from source)
+
+Every runtime and type export of `snapback4/proof` is listed below. Signatures, result fields and failure reasons are generated from TypeScript declarations and their @throws comments; qualification checks this table against the source. Playwright types such as Page and Locator come from playwright-core.
+
+| Signature | Result / fields | Failure reasons |
+|---|---|---|
+| `bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T>` | `Awaited<T>` | deadline expires; work rejection propagates. |
+| `cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown &#124; Promise<unknown>) &#124; undefined>): Promise<void>` | `void` | AggregateError after every cleanup was attempted; each step has a 10-second bound. |
+| `cli(args: readonly string[], options?: ProofOptions): Promise<CliResult>` | `{ code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` | spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. |
+| `command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options?: ProofOptions): Promise<CliResult>` | `{ code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` | spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. |
+| `deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options?: ProofOptions): Promise<CliResult>` | `{ code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` | missing explicit saved source, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. |
+| `fillRN(locator: Locator, text: string): Promise<void>` | `void` | locator ambiguity, disabled/closed control, or Playwright deadline. |
+| `frontPage(page: Page): Promise<Page>` | `Page` | page closed or browser activation failed. |
+| `loadFixtures(directory: string, options: LoadFixturesOptions): Promise<FixtureRow[]>` | `Array of { table: string; row: Record<string, unknown> }` | E_PROOF_USAGE for missing url or invalid timeout; E_AUTH naming attempted credential; catalog, clock, filesystem, row JSON/table or E_FIXTURE_TIME validation failure. |
+| `logsDir(options?: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" &#124; "logsDir">): string` | `string` | None; returns the resolved explicit or cwd/logs path. |
+| `portRange(env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): string` | `string` | partial, invalid or reversed PORT_LO/PORT_HI pair. |
+| `renderedText(page: Page, text: string &#124; RegExp, options?: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean; }): Locator` | `Locator` | None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. |
+| `rollback(dir: string, to: number &#124; "latest", options?: ProofOptions): Promise<CliResult>` | `{ code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` | invalid explicit release target, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. |
+| `sandboxUnavailable(error: unknown): boolean` | `false or true` | None; returns a boolean for recognized sandbox launch errors. |
+| `selectByValue(locator: Locator, value: string): Promise<void>` | `void` | locator ambiguity, unavailable option or Playwright deadline. |
+| `startDev(options?: ProofOptions &#124; readonly string[]): Promise<DevHost>` | `{ personas: string[]; now: () => Promise<number>; url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` | invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. |
+| `startServe(deployDir: string, options?: ProofOptions &#124; readonly string[]): Promise<ProofHost>` | `{ url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` | E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. |
+| `startStatic(dir: string, options?: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost>` | `{ url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` | missing export/index or build script precondition; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. |
+| `startVite(dir: string, options?: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost>` | `{ url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` | missing Vite dependency/configuration; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. |
+| `statusText(page: Page, text?: string &#124; RegExp): Locator` | `Locator` | None at construction; ambiguous text is refused when observed. |
+| `stopChild(child: ChildProcess &#124; Pick<ProofHost, "url" &#124; "stop">, graceMs?: number): Promise<void>` | `void` | invalid handle/deadline, host stop failure or TERM/KILL reap deadline. |
+| `testSource(source: string, options?: TestSourceOptions): Promise<TestSourceResult>` | `{ casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number; code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` | missing source, CLI failure, invalid business-case summary or zero cases without fixtureOnly. |
+| `waitForClock(page: Page, advancedNow: number, options?: { timeoutMs?: number; }): Promise<number>` | `number` | E_PROOF_USAGE for invalid advanced time; missing/disabled witness, signed-out client, page error or page-clock deadline. |
+| `waitForFresh(locator: Locator, previous: string &#124; null, options?: number &#124; { within?: Locator; timeoutMs?: number; }): Promise<string>` | `string` | observation deadline with scoped locator, last text and visible headings; selector ambiguity is propagated. |
+| `waitForServer<T>(read: () => Promise<T>, accepts: (value: T) => boolean, options?: { timeoutMs?: number; label?: string; }): Promise<T>` | `Awaited<T>` | deadline naming label and last value; read or predicate errors propagate. |
+| `withBrowser<T>(options: LaunchOptions & Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" &#124; "logsDir"> & { chromium?: BrowserType; }, proof: (context: BrowserContext) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>` | `Awaited<T>` | E_PROOF_BROWSER for missing playwright-core; invalid deadline, browser launch failure or callback error; bounded context/browser cleanup failure. |
+
+| Exported type | Fields |
+|---|---|
+| `CliResult` | `{ code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` |
+| `DevHost` | `{ personas: string[]; now: () => Promise<number>; url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` |
+| `FixtureRow` | `{ table: string; row: Record<string, unknown> }` |
+| `LoadFixturesOptions` | `url: string; cwd?: string; persona?: string; headers?: Record<string, string>; now?: number; timeoutMs?: number` |
+| `ProofHost` | `{ url: string; pid: number; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; }; stop: () => Promise<void> }` |
+| `ProofOptions` | `cwd?: string; env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv; args?: readonly string[]; timeoutMs?: number; stopTimeoutMs?: number; logsDir?: string; secret?: boolean; detached?: boolean; livenessPipe?: boolean` |
+| `TestSourceOptions` | `fixtureOnly?: boolean; cwd?: string; env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv; args?: readonly string[]; timeoutMs?: number; stopTimeoutMs?: number; logsDir?: string; secret?: boolean; detached?: boolean; livenessPipe?: boolean` |
+| `TestSourceResult` | `{ casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number; code: number &#124; null; signal: NodeJS.Signals &#124; null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string; } }` |
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing.md b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing.md
index 2755fdd36b..41a8b070c5 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/testing.md

@@ -73,7 +73,7 @@ what later cases see. The complete vocabulary is `as`, `op`, `args`, `expect`,
 {"as":"bob","op":"cardsOn","args":{"board":"board-alice"},"count":0}

diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
index c93870a45c..47bf43b64c 100644
--- a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -1,5 +1,15 @@
 # Snapback 4 behavior changes
 
+## 0.4.32
+
+- Clock advances coalesce arbitrarily many crossed windows; the 10,000-window cap applies only to `--each-boundary`. Skipped-window replies retain exact counts and preview up to 32 replay commands per schedule.
+- Witness mismatches name argument keys; full armed/actual values live in a private mode-0600 proof log. waitForFresh adds substring advice only within explicit scope and names visible headings on timeout.
+- Proof fixtures validate their URL first and identify refused personas or token fingerprints. Missing frontend output names the project's build:web/build script; withBrowser checks playwright-core first.
+- Clock replay commands carry the live dev port. Each-boundary reasons name every window; configured offsets remain exact. waitForClock waits for the page client's advanced clock sample.
+- Stale online clients after dev reset halt with E_DEV_RESET and instructions to reopen; outstanding writes retain unknown outcomes and are never retried automatically.
+- Fixture aliases support zero-based array paths such as $plans.0.id. Test output explains its columns and explicit --as sweep precedence.
+- The testing guide includes a complete production-account day-two proof and a source-generated proof API table. The wire guide opens with runnable literal envelopes, seed/reset forms and credential headers. Packed qualification runs the examples unchanged.
+
 ## 0.4.31
 
 - Existing stores keep their rows, ids, schema and captured `read through` authority. No automatic data repair or audience expansion occurs. Regenerate declarations and rebuild web, native and Exact clients to pick up the runtime and client changes below; review new correctness notes before deploying edited schemas.
diff --git a/snapback4/scripts/generate-proof-api.mjs b/snapback4/scripts/generate-proof-api.mjs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..7e1690cf96
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/scripts/generate-proof-api.mjs
@@ -0,0 +1,43 @@
+// The proof export census, signatures, result fields and failures come from TypeScript.
+import ts from 'typescript';
+import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { resolve } from 'node:path';
+const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('../',import.meta.url));
+export function proofApi() {
+  const config=ts.readConfigFile(resolve(root,'packages/snapback4/tsconfig.build.json'),ts.sys.readFile);
+  const parsed=ts.parseJsonConfigFileContent(config.config,ts.sys,resolve(root,'packages/snapback4'));
+  const program=ts.createProgram(parsed.fileNames,parsed.options), checker=program.getTypeChecker();
+  const source=program.getSourceFile(resolve(root,'packages/snapback4/src/proof.ts'));
+  const module=checker.getSymbolAtLocation(source);
+  const fmt=ts.TypeFormatFlags.NoTruncation | ts.TypeFormatFlags.UseAliasDefinedOutsideCurrentScope;
+  const show=type=>checker.typeToString(type,source,fmt).replace(/import\("[^"]+"\)\./g,'');
+  const cell=text=>text.replace(/\|/g,'&#124;').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
+  const fields=type=>{
+    type=checker.getAwaitedType(type)??type;
+    if(checker.isArrayType(type)) return `Array of ${fields(checker.getTypeArguments(type)[0])}`;
+    if(type.isUnion()) return type.types.map(fields).join(' or ');
+    const name=show(type);
+    if(!['ProofHost','DevHost','CliResult','TestSourceResult','FixtureRow'].includes(name)) return name;
+    return `{ ${checker.getPropertiesOfType(type).map(p=>`${p.name}${p.flags&ts.SymbolFlags.Optional?'?':''}: ${show(checker.getTypeOfSymbolAtLocation(p,source))}`).join('; ')} }`;
+  };
+  const functions=[],types=[];
+  for(let symbol of checker.getExportsOfModule(module).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
+    const name=symbol.name;
+    if(symbol.flags&ts.SymbolFlags.Alias) symbol=checker.getAliasedSymbol(symbol);
+    const type=checker.getTypeOfSymbolAtLocation(symbol,source), signatures=type.getCallSignatures();
+    if(signatures.length){
+      const failures=symbol.getJsDocTags(checker).filter(tag=>tag.name==='throws').map(tag=>tag.text?.map(part=>part.text).join('')??'').join('; ');
+      if(!failures) throw Error(`proof export ${name} needs source @throws documentation`);
+      functions.push(`| ${signatures.map(sig=>'`'+cell(name+checker.signatureToString(sig,source,fmt))+'`').join('<br>')} | \`${cell(fields(checker.getReturnTypeOfSignature(signatures[0])))}\` | ${cell(failures)} |`);
+    } else {
+      const declared=checker.getDeclaredTypeOfSymbol(symbol);
+      types.push(`| \`${name}\` | \`${cell(fields(declared)===''+name ? checker.getPropertiesOfType(declared).map(p=>`${p.name}${p.flags&ts.SymbolFlags.Optional?'?':''}: ${show(checker.getTypeOfSymbolAtLocation(p,source))}`).join('; ') : fields(declared))}\` |`);
+    }
+  }
+  return '## Proof API (generated from source)\n\nEvery runtime and type export of `snapback4/proof` is listed below. Signatures, result fields and failure reasons are generated from TypeScript declarations and their @throws comments; qualification checks this table against the source. Playwright types such as Page and Locator come from playwright-core.\n\n| Signature | Result / fields | Failure reasons |\n|---|---|---|\n'+functions.join('\n')+'\n\n| Exported type | Fields |\n|---|---|\n'+types.join('\n')+'\n';
+}
+const path=resolve(root,'crates/snapback4-lang/src/guide/proof_api.md');
+const output=proofApi();
+if(process.argv.includes('--check')) {if(readFileSync(path,'utf8')!==output) throw Error('proof API table is stale; run node scripts/generate-proof-api.mjs');}
+else writeFileSync(path,output);

//! Bounded, read-only evidence from the store's own commit clock.
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

const SKIPPED_PREVIEW: usize = 32;
const TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 5_000;
const RECENT_COMMITS: usize = 64;
const TABLE_BYTES: usize = 4096;
const TABLE_NAMES: usize = 8;
const REPORT_ERROR_BYTES: usize = 1024;
// Reserve one MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host encoder.
const REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET: usize = crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING - (1 << 20);

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — inspect a fixed recent commit window, never application row values.
pub fn ahead(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> {
    // Reverse primary-key traversal: no full scan, sort, row payloads or retained
    // historical stamp set. octet_length(column) uses SQLite record metadata;
    // unlike a text length/cast/substr, it need not load oversized payloads.
    let mut query = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT at, CASE WHEN at > ?1 AND octet_length(touched) <= ?2 THEN CAST(touched AS BLOB) END,
         octet_length(touched) FROM _commits ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?3")?;
    let mut rows = query.query(rusqlite::params![now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS), TABLE_BYTES as i64, RECENT_COMMITS as i64])?;
    let mut lead = 0;
    let mut tables = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut omitted = false;
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let at: i64 = row.get(0)?;
        if at <= now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS) { continue; }
        lead = lead.max(at.saturating_sub(now));
        let length: i64 = row.get(2)?;
        if length > TABLE_BYTES as i64 { omitted = true; continue; }
        let bytes: Vec<u8> = row.get(1)?;
        match serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<String>>(&bytes) {
            Ok(names) => for name in names {
                if tables.contains(&name) { continue; }
                if tables.len() < TABLE_NAMES && name.len() <= 128 { tables.insert(name); }
                else { omitted = true; }
            },
            Err(_) => omitted = true,
        }
    }
    if lead == 0 { return Ok(None); }
    let mut names = tables.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if omitted { names.push("additional tables omitted".into()); }
    if names.is_empty() { names.push("no table names recorded".into()); }
    Ok(Some(format!(
        "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD: recent commit stamps lead the current clock by up to {lead}ms (latest {RECENT_COMMITS} commits; tables: {}); use a fresh store per offset test ({}), or restart with the same --clock-offset (or dev --resume-clock) to continue",
        names.join(", "), crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())))
}

/// UTC calendar text for Unix milliseconds: schedule boundaries are UTC.
pub fn utc(ms: i64) -> String {
    let (days, rem) = (ms.div_euclid(86_400_000), ms.rem_euclid(86_400_000));
    // Civil-from-days (proleptic Gregorian), exact for the I53 range.
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z", rem / 3_600_000, rem % 3_600_000 / 60_000, rem % 60_000 / 1_000)
}

/// A positive duration in the units `clock advance` accepts, rounded up to a second.
pub fn span(ms: i64) -> String {
    let seconds = (ms.max(0) + 999) / 1_000;
    let (d, h, m, s) = (seconds / 86_400, seconds % 86_400 / 3_600, seconds % 3_600 / 60, seconds % 60);
    let parts: Vec<String> = [(d, "d"), (h, "h"), (m, "m"), (s, "s")].into_iter().filter(|(n, _)| *n > 0).map(|(n, unit)| format!("{n}{unit}")).collect();
    if parts.is_empty() { "0s".into() } else { parts.join("") }
}

/// Each schedule's saved boundary, in declaration order.
pub fn saved_boundaries(engine: &Engine) -> Vec<Option<i64>> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter()
        .map(|schedule| {
            let shape = engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten();
            if shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|shape| *shape != snapback4_core::value::digest(schedule)) { return None; }
            engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten().and_then(|value| value.parse().ok())
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Preflight is bounded before the clock or worker leases change.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — only explicit execution enumerates all crossed windows; coalesced reports keep a bounded preview.
pub fn crossed_boundaries(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Result<Vec<(usize, i64)>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    let mut boundaries = Vec::new();
    let mut report_bytes = 0usize;
    for (index, schedule) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate() {
        // JSON may escape every error byte; count that bound per schedule.
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add(2048 + 6 * (REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3) + 8 * (schedule.name.len() + schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule report exceeds its byte budget; reduce the declared schedules")); }
        if held { continue; }
        let Some(old) = before.get(index).copied().flatten() else { continue; };
        let end = schedule.boundary(now);
        if old >= end { continue; }
        let periods = schedule.periods_between(old, end);
        if each_boundary && periods > (10_000 - boundaries.len()) as i64 {
            return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("--each-boundary crosses more than 10000 schedule windows; use a smaller duration or omit --each-boundary to coalesce"));
        }
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add((if each_boundary { periods as usize } else { (periods as usize).min(SKIPPED_PREVIEW + 1) }).saturating_mul(1024 + 8 * schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule window report exceeds its byte budget; use a smaller duration")); }
        if !each_boundary { continue; }
        let mut boundary = schedule.next(old);
        while boundary <= end {
            boundaries.push((index, boundary));
            boundary = schedule.next(boundary);
        }
    }
    boundaries.sort_by_key(|(index, at)| (*at, *index));
    Ok(boundaries)
}

fn period(schedule: &snapback4_core::schema::Schedule) -> String {
    schedule.describe()
}

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool, port: Option<u16>) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            if each_boundary {
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired {} windows: {}", windows.len(), windows.iter().map(|window| format!("{} to {}", utc(window["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap()), utc(window["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")).into();
            }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            if !each_boundary { entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into(); }
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'{}", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args), port.map(|port| format!(" --port {port}")).unwrap_or_default()).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > current) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: its saved boundary {} is ahead of the clock {} (a dev restart resets the clock offset; the store keeps the boundary). Advance {} more, restart with dev --resume-clock, or test on dev --fresh",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT name, state, rows_written, substr(error, 1, ?1), octet_length(error) > ?1, id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 64")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([REPORT_ERROR_BYTES as i64])?;
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let name: String = row.get(0)?;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error: Option<String> = row.get(3)?;
        let truncated = row.get::<_, Option<bool>>(4)?.unwrap_or(false);
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":row.get::<_, String>(5)?, "state":row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
            "rowsWritten":row.get::<_, i64>(2)?, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
        state.advancing = false;
        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
    }
}

fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(now, index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work(shared, true, 0)?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

pub(super) fn clock_status(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let result = (|| {
        let (epoch, reader) = shared.reader()?;
        let note = crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&reader, shared.now());
        lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
        note
    })();
    match result {
        Ok(note) => { let now = shared.now(); respond(request, 200, json!({"storeAhead":note,"now":now,"skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms()})) },
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use super::super::tests::Host;

    #[test]
    fn kit3_interrupted_clock_drain_refuses_with_clock_evidence_and_queued_work() {
        const CHILD: &str = "SNAPBACK4_KIT3_DRAIN_CHILD";
        if std::env::var_os(CHILD).is_none() {
            let result = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args(["--exact", "serve::maintenance::tests::kit3_interrupted_clock_drain_refuses_with_clock_evidence_and_queued_work", "--nocapture"])
                .env(CHILD, "1").output().unwrap();
            assert!(result.status.success(), "{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));
            return;
        }
        // Signal state is isolated in this one-test child, never the suite.
        crate::lifecycle::install("dev").unwrap(); crate::lifecycle::managed_cleanup();
        let host = Host::with_store(true, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),
            "use personas alice\ntable rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n");
        let shared = host.shared.clone();
        let before = shared.now();
        let id = lock(&shared.engine).enqueue_job("tick", Default::default(), before).unwrap();
        let (entered, resume) = shared.engine.pause_next_background(false);
        let address = host.address;
        let client = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let payload = r#"{"duration":"1m"}"#;
            super::super::tests::exchange(address, &format!("POST /dev/clock/advance HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len()))
        });
        entered.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap();
        unsafe { libc::raise(libc::SIGTERM); }
        resume.send(()).unwrap();
        let response = client.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::super::tests::status(&response), 500, "{response}");
        let reply = super::super::tests::body(&response);
        assert_eq!(reply["denied"]["code"], "E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "{reply}");
        assert!(reply["now"].as_i64().unwrap() >= before+60_000 && reply["skewMs"].is_i64());
        assert_eq!(lock(&shared.engine).job(&id).unwrap().unwrap().state, snapback4_core::JobState::Queued);
    }

  let value = BigInt(now);

/** The id a stable key names, in the `mintId()` form: the same key gives the
 * same id on every client and host. For fixtures, seeds and replays keyed by a
 * label (`idFrom("seed:post-17")`), as a write id or a fixture row's id. It is a
 * name, not a secret: anyone who knows the key knows the id. A derived id sorts
 * before every minted id, as if minted at the epoch. */
export function idFrom(key: string): string {
  if (typeof key !== "string" || key === "") throw new TypeError("idFrom: the key must be a non-empty string");
  const digest = sha256(utf8(JSON.stringify(["snapback4:id:v1", key])));
  // 80 bits of hash under a zero time: a derived row never crowds a `by .id`
  // window of minted rows, and the first ten digits read as zeros.
  let value = 0n;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) value = (value << 8n) | BigInt(digest[i]!);
  let out = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    out = ALPHABET[Number(value & 31n)] + out;
    value >>= 5n;
  }
  return out;
}

/// What `POST /dev/reset` was asked to do: the body is empty, a `/seed`
/// array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}` with either key optional.
#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
struct ResetRequest { seed: Vec<Json>, clock: bool }

fn reset_request(body: &str) -> Result<ResetRequest, Refusal> {
    const SHAPE: &str = "reset takes no body, a /seed array [{table, row}, ..], or {\"seed\": [..], \"clock\": \"reset\"} with either key optional";
    if body.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(ResetRequest::default()); }
    match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(seed)) => Ok(ResetRequest { seed, clock: false }),
        Ok(Json::Object(fields)) => {
            let mut request = ResetRequest::default();
            for (key, value) in fields {
                match (key.as_str(), value) {
                    ("seed", Json::Array(seed)) => request.seed = seed,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "reset" => request.clock = true,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "keep" => request.clock = false,
                    ("clock", other) => return Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("reset's clock is \"reset\" (back to wall time) or \"keep\" (the default), not {other}"))),
                    (key, _) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{SHAPE}; got key {key:?}"))),
                }
            }
            Ok(request)
        }
        _ => Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", SHAPE)),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — one transaction: empty, new identity, reseed; then the host's caches follow.
pub(super) fn reset(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.personas || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {

        Err(refusal) => return refuse(request, refusal),

    // Serialize with clock advances, then take the engine before the clock:
    // the order clock advance and worker claims use.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);

//! Schedule boundaries: fixed periods (`every 2m`, `every 1d at 08:00`) and
//! UTC calendar months (`every 1 month at day 1 09:00`, `every 1 year`).
//! The scheduler, the dev clock and `doctor` use only these methods.
use super::Schedule;

pub(super) fn is_zero_months(months: &u32) -> bool { *months == 0 }

/// The latest offset a calendar schedule may name: the end of day 28.
pub const CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS: u64 = 28 * 86_400_000;
/// At most once a century.
pub const CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT: u32 = 1_200;

impl Schedule {
    /// The most recent period boundary at or before `now`.
    pub fn boundary(&self, now: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 {
            let n = i64::from(self.months);
            let mut month = crate::calendar::month_of(now).div_euclid(n) * n;
            if self.month_boundary(month) > now { month -= n; }
            return self.month_boundary(month);
        }
        let every = self.every_ms as i64;
        let offset = self.offset_ms as i64;
        (now - offset).div_euclid(every) * every + offset
    }

    /// The boundary one period before `boundary`: the `periodStart` of the
    /// period that ends there.
    pub fn previous(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) - i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_sub(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// The boundary one period after `boundary`.
    pub fn next(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) + i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_add(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// How many boundaries lie in `(from, to]`, for two boundaries of this schedule.
    pub fn periods_between(&self, from: i64, to: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return (crate::calendar::month_of(to) - crate::calendar::month_of(from)).div_euclid(i64::from(self.months)); }
        (to - from) / (self.every_ms as i64).max(1)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — exact: the month start plus the offset in i128,
    // never an endpoint substituted at the i53 edges (only ±285,000 years out).
    fn month_boundary(&self, month: i64) -> i64 {
        let days = crate::calendar::days_from_civil(1970 + i128::from(month).div_euclid(12), i128::from(month).rem_euclid(12) + 1, 1);
        let at = days * i128::from(crate::calendar::DAY_MS) + i128::from(self.offset_ms);
        at.clamp(i128::from(i64::MIN), i128::from(i64::MAX)) as i64
    }

    /// `every 1 month at day 1 09:00 UTC`, `every 1d at 03:00 UTC`, `every 2m`.
    pub fn describe(&self) -> String {
        let clock = |ms: u64| format!("{:02}:{:02}", ms / 3_600_000, ms % 3_600_000 / 60_000);
        if self.months > 0 {
            let (n, unit) = if self.months % 12 == 0 { (self.months / 12, "year") } else { (self.months, "month") };
            let day = self.offset_ms / 86_400_000 + 1;
            return format!("every {n} {unit}{} at day {day} {} UTC", if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, clock(self.offset_ms % 86_400_000));
        }
        let every = self.every_ms;
        let unit = [(86_400_000, "d"), (3_600_000, "h"), (60_000, "m"), (1_000, "s"), (1, "ms")].into_iter().find(|(n, _)| every % n == 0).unwrap_or((1, "ms"));
        let at = if self.offset_ms > 0 { format!(" at {} UTC", clock(self.offset_ms)) } else { String::new() };
        format!("every {}{}{at}", every / unit.0, unit.1)
    }

    /// The period and offset fit one of the two shapes.
    pub fn valid(&self) -> bool {
        if self.months > 0 {
            return self.every_ms == 0 && self.months <= CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT && self.offset_ms < CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS;
        }
        self.every_ms != 0 && self.every_ms <= i64::MAX as u64 && self.offset_ms < self.every_ms
    }
}

import { DeliveryQueue, memoryDelivery } from "./delivery.ts";
import type { Refusal } from "./contract.ts";
import type { DeliveryRefused } from "./delivery-state.ts";

export function onlineDelivery(env: {
  url: string; fetch: typeof fetch; mint(): string;
  offline?(): void;
  ready(): boolean; current(): boolean;
  headers(): Record<string, string>; revision(): number;
  floor(): number; store(): string | undefined;
  observe(json: Record<string, unknown>, floor: number): void;
  obsolete(why: Refusal | undefined, revision: number): Promise<boolean>;
  refresh(): Promise<unknown> | null;
  onRefused?: ((event: DeliveryRefused) => void) | undefined;
}) {
  return new DeliveryQueue({ local: false, storage: memoryDelivery(), mint: env.mint,
    ready: env.ready, onOffline: () => env.offline?.(), onRefused: event => env.onRefused?.(event),
    send: async (mutation, body, signal) => {
      if (env.refresh()) await env.refresh();
      if (!env.ready() || signal.aborted) return { json: {}, accepted: false, deferred: true };
      const revision = env.revision(), floor = env.floor(), store = env.store();
      const response = await env.fetch(`${env.url}/m/${encodeURIComponent(mutation)}`, {
        method: "POST", headers: env.headers(), body: JSON.stringify({ ...body, store_id: store, retry_write: true }), signal,
      });
      const json = await response.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (signal.aborted || !env.current()) throw new Error("obsolete delivery response");
      const why = (json.why ?? json.denied) as Refusal | undefined;
      // Missing heads are uncertain, never inferred from a receipt's historical seq.
      const validHead = Number.isSafeInteger(json.head) && Number(json.head) >= 0;
      env.observe(validHead ? json : { ...json, seq: undefined, head: undefined }, floor);
      if (!env.current()) return { json: { ...json, denied: { code: typeof json.store_id === "string" && store && json.store_id !== store ? "E_STORE_REPLACED" : "E_STORE_RESTORE" } }, accepted: false };
      if (why?.code === "E_AUTH") return { json, accepted: false, obsoleteAuth: await env.obsolete(why, revision) };
      return { json, accepted: validHead && typeof json.store_id === "string" && response.status < 500 };
    },
  });
}

 succeeded in 0ms:
import { refreshSession } from "./auth.ts";
import { isReadToken } from "./read-token.ts";
import type { Client, Refusal, Session } from "./contract.ts";
import { readResponse, transportRefusal } from "./http-response.ts";

export const readOnlyRefusal = (code = "E_READ_ONLY"): Refusal => ({ code, family: "auth", guide: "read-only",
  message: ({ E_READ_ONLY: "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes",
    E_READ_ONLY_REFRESH: "read sessions cannot refresh; sign in again or narrow a full session again",
    E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE: "a read session cannot open a device; use the online read-only client",
    E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL: "readOnly needs a server-verified read session, without a persona",
    E_READ_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED: "this server cannot verify read sessions; upgrade it before using readOnly" } as Record<string, string>)[code] ?? code });
export function refuseReadDevice(session?: Session | null, token?: string): void {
  // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.4 — token-based admission works offline and ignores forged scope fields.
  if (isReadToken(session?.token ?? token)) throw Object.assign(new Error("E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE: a read session cannot open a device"), { why: readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE") });
}
// Apply the same admission check to every device rotation, including a late
// successor. Refuse before adoption or persistence even if scope was forged.
export function refreshDeviceSession(...args: Parameters<typeof refreshSession>): ReturnType<typeof refreshSession> {
  return refreshSession(...args).then(result => { if (result.ok) refuseReadDevice(result.session); return result; });
}
export function validateReadOptions(options: { readOnly?: boolean; persona?: string; device?: unknown }): void {
  const code = options.readOnly && options.device ? "E_READ_ONLY_DEVICE" : options.readOnly && options.persona ? "E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL" : undefined;
  if (code) throw Object.assign(new Error(`${code}: ${readOnlyRefusal(code).message}`), { why: readOnlyRefusal(code) });
}

const readiness = new WeakMap<Client, () => Promise<Refusal | undefined>>();
export const registerReadVerification = (client: Client, verify: () => Promise<Refusal | undefined>) => { readiness.set(client, verify); };
export const verifyReadClient = (client: Client) => readiness.get(client)?.() ?? Promise.resolve(undefined);

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.4 — nothing passes this gate, including
// exported asset credentials, before the exact bearer/revision/store is verified.
export class ReadScopeGate {
  private accepted: string | undefined;
  private pending: { key: string; work: Promise<Refusal | undefined> } | undefined;
  private failed: { key: string; why: Refusal } | undefined;
  constructor(private url: string, private transport: typeof fetch,
    private current: () => { token: string | undefined; revision: number; store: string | undefined; closed: boolean },
    private accept: (json: Record<string, unknown>) => void, private signal: AbortSignal) {}
  private key() { const c = this.current(); return JSON.stringify([c.token, c.revision, c.store]); }
  get verified() { return !this.current().closed && this.accepted === this.key(); }
  get refusal() { return this.failed?.key === this.key() ? this.failed.why : undefined; }
  verify(): Promise<Refusal | undefined> {
    if (this.verified) return Promise.resolve(undefined);
    const key = this.key();
    if (this.failed?.key === key) return Promise.resolve(this.failed.why);
    if (this.pending?.key === key) return this.pending.work;
    const c = this.current();
    this.accepted = undefined;
    const work = (async () => {
      let why: Refusal | undefined;
      try {
        if (c.closed || !c.token) return readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
        const response = await this.transport(`${this.url}/probe?scope=1`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${c.token}` }, signal: this.signal });
        const json = await readResponse(response);
        if (key !== this.key() || this.current().closed) return { code: "E_AUTH", family: "auth", message: "the read credential changed during verification" };
        if (json.denied) why = json.denied as Refusal;
        else if (!response.ok || json.ok !== true || !["full", "read"].includes(String(json.scope)) || typeof json.store_id !== "string") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_UNSUPPORTED");
        else if (json.scope !== "read") why = readOnlyRefusal("E_READ_ONLY_CREDENTIAL");
        else { this.accept(json); this.accepted = this.key(); }
      } catch (error) { why = transportRefusal(error); }
      if (why && key === this.key() && !why.retryable) this.failed = { key, why };
      return why;
    })();
    this.pending = { key, work };
    void work.finally(() => { if (this.pending?.work === work) this.pending = undefined; });
    return work;
  }
}

/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
        if options.flags.contains_key("release") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
        }
        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let (address, instance) = crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?;
        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
        let (status, json) = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)).map_err(|error| {
            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
        })?;
        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
        }
        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
        Ok(json.to_string())
    })();
    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod isolated_tests {
    #[test]
    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {

    // @ref LLP 3000.001.000#workers — keep live supervised bearers valid until their orderly clock-revision replacement.
    pub fn advance_worker_leases_at(&mut self, milliseconds: i64, before: i64) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let supervised: bool = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _meta WHERE key = 'worker:session-family' OR key LIKE 'worker:family:%')", [], |row| row.get(0))?;

        // Advancing the dev clock cannot extend a claim past its admitting family.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _requests SET lease_until = CASE WHEN family IS NULL OR principal_generation IS NULL OR admitted_expires_at IS NULL THEN lease_until + ?1 ELSE MIN(lease_until + ?1, COALESCE((SELECT MAX(s.expires_at) FROM _sessions s WHERE s.principal = _requests.principal AND COALESCE(s.family, s.token_digest) = _requests.family), lease_until)) END WHERE state = 'running' AND lease_until IS NOT NULL", [milliseconds])?;

    /// A claim whose lease ran out goes back to the queue: the worker died
    /// or stalled, and the attempt it took stays counted.
    pub fn requeue_expired_effects(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let changed = expire(tx.conn(), now)?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(changed)
    }

    /// A bounded maintenance pass; settlement still checks the live lease.
    pub fn requeue_expired_effects_for(&mut self, now: i64, budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let changed = expire_batch(tx.conn(), now, 256, budget)?;

        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
        let run = tx.conn().query_row(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM _outbox WHERE id = ?1"), [id], read_run).optional()?.ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_EFFECT_UNKNOWN", message: "no effect with this id is available".into() })?;
