    /// Queue the job of every schedule whose period boundary the clock has
    /// crossed since it last fired, with the period that ended there as
    /// `periodStart`/`periodEnd` when the job declares them. The boundary
    /// fired for is committed with the job row, so a restart never fires
    /// twice for one period; a schedule seen for the first time fires from
    /// its next boundary. Returns the names fired.
    pub fn fire_schedules(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, 0..self.schema.schedules.len(), None)
    }

    /// One atomic schedule boundary, so the host can release its writer between
    /// schedules. A changed/shortened schema is revisited on the next tick.
    pub fn fire_schedule_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), None)
    }

    /// Admit one historical period on the current clock. In particular an
    /// action's deadline begins at admission, not at the historical boundary.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — period identity and admission time are distinct during explicit catch-up.
    pub fn fire_schedule_boundary_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize, boundary: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let schedule = self.schema.schedules.get(index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("unknown schedule index".into()))?;
        if schedule.boundary(boundary) != boundary || boundary > schedule.boundary(now) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Input("schedule window must end at an elapsed boundary".into()));
        }
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), Some(boundary))
    }

    fn fire_schedule_range(&mut self, now: i64, range: std::ops::Range<usize>, selected: Option<i64>) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fired = Vec::new();
        for schedule in self.schema.schedules.get(range).unwrap_or(&[]).to_vec() {
            let boundary = selected.unwrap_or_else(|| schedule.boundary(now));
            let key = format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape_key = format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape = digest(&schedule);
            let program = self.programs.get(&schedule.target).filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore its target before firing it", schedule.name, schedule.target)))?.clone();
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action && !schedule.service.as_deref().is_some_and(crate::identity::is_service) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into()));
            }
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job && schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled job runs as the system, not a service seat".into()));
            }
            let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
            let previous_shape: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&shape_key], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            if previous_shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|previous| previous != &shape) {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
            let last: Option<i64> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&key], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|value| value.parse().ok());
            if last.is_some_and(|last| last >= boundary) {
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            let Some(_) = last else {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            };
            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
            if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") {
                args.insert("periodStart".to_owned(), Value::Int(schedule.previous(boundary)));
            }
            if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") {
                args.insert("periodEnd".to_owned(), Value::Int(boundary));
            }
            let id = crate::ids::mint(now, &mut self.minter)?;
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let principal = schedule.service.as_deref().expect("checked service seat");
                validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut args)?;
                super::actions::authorize(&program, principal)?;
                let raw = serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&args));
                let context = Context::new(principal, args).at(now);
                // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.2 — the boundary and service-seat admission are one commit.
                super::actions::insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, &program, &id, &context, &raw, true, now)?;
            } else {
                #[cfg(feature = "native")]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, self.native.as_deref().map(super::native::Module::digest));
                #[cfg(not(feature = "native"))]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, None);
                insert_job(tx.conn(), &id, &schedule.target, &args, &program_hash, now)?;
            }
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", params![key, boundary.to_string()])?;
            tx.commit()?;
            fired.push(schedule.name.clone());
        }
        Ok(fired)

export { loadFixtures, type FixtureRow, type LoadFixturesOptions } from "./proof-fixtures.ts";
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — the packaged proof kit owns hosts; builders own assertions.
import { mkdirSync, openSync, closeSync, writeSync } from "node:fs";

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { resolveBinary } from "./bin-path.ts";
import { bounded, deadline, logged, logText, portRange, ready, stopChild, type ProofHost, type ProofOptions, logsDir } from "./proof-process.ts";
export { bounded, cleanup, stopChild, portRange } from "./proof-process.ts";
export type { ProofHost, ProofOptions } from "./proof-process.ts";

const fetchIdentity = async (url: string, timeoutMs: number) => {
  const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
  return { response, body: await response.json() as Record<string, unknown> };
};
async function nativeHost(verb: "dev" | "serve", prefix: string[], options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost & { personas?: string[] }> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, id = randomUUID();
  const ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (!args.includes("--port")) args.push("--port", ports);
  const run = logged(resolveBinary(), [verb, ...prefix, ...args], { ...options, env: { ...env, SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID: id, SNAPBACK4_SUPERVISOR_PID: String(process.pid) } });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_READY verb=(dev|serve) generation=\d+ url=(http:\/\/[^ ]+) pid=(\d+)(?: |$)/);
      if (!match) return;
      if (match[1] !== verb || Number(match[3]) !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof ready identity mismatch");
      return match[2];
    }, timeout);
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/auth/config`, timeout);
    if (!response.ok || body.proof_id !== id) throw Error("proof served identity mismatch");
    let personas: string[] = [];
    if (verb === "dev") {
      const status = await fetchIdentity(`${url}/dev/status`, timeout);
      if (status.body.pid !== run.child.pid) throw Error("proof dev PID mismatch");
      if (!Array.isArray(status.body.personas) || !status.body.personas.every(name => typeof name === "string")) throw Error("proof dev personas missing");
      personas = status.body.personas;
    }
    return { url, ...(verb === "dev" ? { personas } : {}), pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
export interface DevHost extends ProofHost {
  personas: string[];
  /** Read the current authoritative development time, including all clock advances. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
}
/** The array form preserves the former startDev(args) recipe. */
/** @throws invalid port range or conflicting memory flags; child exits, readiness deadline, PID/served identity mismatch, or unavailable dev clock. */
export function startDev(options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<DevHost> {
  const opts: ProofOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions;
  const args = [...(opts.args ?? [])];
  const persistent = args.some(arg => arg === "--store" || arg.startsWith("--store=") || arg === "--no-memory");
  if (persistent && args.includes("--memory")) throw new TypeError("proof startDev: --memory conflicts with --store/--no-memory");
  // --no-memory is a proof option; native dev already persists by default.
  const host = nativeHost("dev", [], { ...opts, args: [...args.filter(arg => arg !== "--no-memory"), ...(persistent || args.includes("--memory") ? [] : ["--memory"])] });
  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable");
    return body.now;
  } }));
}
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ProofOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ProofHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  return nativeHost("serve", [resolve(options && !Array.isArray(options) ? (options as ProofOptions).cwd ?? "." : ".", deployDir)],
    Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ProofOptions);
}
async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {
  const env = { ...process.env, ...options.env }, ports = portRange(env), timeout = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000);
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".");
  const directory = resolve(cwd, dir);
  // Vite is resolved from the app, never the proof package's development dependencies.
  const vite = kind === "vite" ? createRequire(resolve(directory, "package.json")).resolve("vite") : "";
  const run = logged(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("./proof-host.js", import.meta.url)), kind, directory, ports, vite], { ...options, env, detached: process.platform !== "win32", livenessPipe: true });
  try {
    const url = await ready(run, line => {
      const match = line.match(/^SNAPBACK4_PROOF_READY (\{.*\})$/);
      if (!match) return;
      const data = JSON.parse(match[1]!);
      if (data.pid !== run.child.pid || data.kind !== kind) throw Error("proof frontend PID mismatch");
      return data.url as string;
    }, timeout);
    const response = await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeout) });
    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
    await response.body?.cancel();
    return { url, pid: run.child.pid!, logs: run.logs, stop: () => stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)) };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
}
/** @throws missing export/index or build script precondition; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startStatic(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("static", dir, options); }
/** @throws missing Vite dependency/configuration; exhausted ports, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export function startVite(dir: string, options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<ProofHost> { return frontend("vite", dir, options); }

export interface CliResult { code: number | null; signal: NodeJS.Signals | null; stdout: string; stderr: string; logs: ProofHost["logs"] }
/** Waits for close (stdio drained), never merges the two streams. Nonzero is returned for refusal assertions. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export function cli(args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  return command(process.execPath, [fileURLToPath(new URL("../bin/snapback4.js", import.meta.url)), ...args], { ...options, secret: options.secret || args[0] === "token" });
}
/** A bounded directly spawned build/tool command, with the same file logs. */
/** @throws spawn error or command deadline; nonzero exit is returned in code, not thrown. */
export async function command(binary: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}): Promise<CliResult> {
  const run = logged(binary, args, { ...options, env: { ...process.env, ...options.env } });
  try {
    await bounded(run.closed, deadline(options.timeoutMs, 120_000), "proof cli close");
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    return { code: run.child.exitCode, signal: run.child.signalCode, stdout: run.stdout(), stderr: logText(run.logs.stderr), logs: run.logs };
  } catch (error) { await stopChild(run.child, deadline(options.stopTimeoutMs, 5000)); throw error; }
  finally { run.child.stdout?.destroy(); } // Secret capture must not pin the driver after a deadline.
}
async function successful(args: string[], options: ProofOptions) {
  const result = await cli(args, options);
  if (result.code !== 0) {
    const stdout = result.stdout.trimEnd();
    const tail = stdout.length > 4000 ? `…${stdout.slice(-4000)}` : stdout;
    throw Error(`proof CLI failed (${result.code}): ${result.stderr}; stdout tail: ${tail || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stderr}`);
  }
  return result;
}
/** @throws missing explicit saved source, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function deploy(dir: string, schema: string, options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (typeof schema !== "string" || !schema) throw Error(`proof deploy requires an explicit saved schema file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(schema) ?? String(schema)}: did npm run day2:proof pass versions/v1?`);
  return successful(["deploy", dir, "--schema", schema, ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
/** @throws invalid explicit release target, CLI failure, spawn error or deadline. */
export function rollback(dir: string, to: number | "latest", options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  if (to !== "latest" && (!Number.isSafeInteger(to) || to < 1)) throw Error(`proof rollback requires an explicit immutable release target; got ${JSON.stringify(to) ?? String(to)}`);
  return successful(["rollback", dir, "--to", String(to), ...(options.args ?? [])], options);
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — saved-source proof results expose authored cases separately from query sweeps.
export interface TestSourceOptions extends ProofOptions { fixtureOnly?: boolean }
export interface TestSourceResult extends CliResult { casesRun: number; casesSkipped: number }
/** @throws missing source, CLI failure, invalid business-case summary or zero cases without fixtureOnly. */
export function testSource(source: string, options: TestSourceOptions = {}): Promise<TestSourceResult> {
  if (typeof source !== "string" || !source) throw Error(`proof testSource requires an explicit saved source file or directory; got ${JSON.stringify(source) ?? String(source)}: pass versions/v1`);
  const { fixtureOnly = false, ...proofOptions } = options;
  return successful(["test", "--source", source, ...(proofOptions.args ?? [])], proofOptions).then(result => {
    const summaries = [...result.stdout.matchAll(/^SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES (\{.*\})$/gm)];
    if (summaries.length !== 1) throw Error(`proof testSource received no unique business-case summary; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    let cases: { run: number; skipped: number };
    try { cases = JSON.parse(summaries[0]![1]!) as { run: number; skipped: number }; }
    catch { throw Error(`proof testSource received a malformed business-case summary: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`); }
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cases.run) || cases.run < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(cases.skipped) || cases.skipped < 0) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource received invalid business-case counts: ${summaries[0]![0]}; logs: ${result.logs.stdout}`);
    }
    if (cases.run === 0 && !fixtureOnly) {
      throw Error(`proof testSource ran no business cases (${cases.skipped} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl for this saved version, or pass { fixtureOnly: true } when fixture loading alone is the intended proof; stdout tail: ${result.stdout.trimEnd().slice(-4000) || "(empty)"}`);
    }
    return { ...result, casesRun: cases.run, casesSkipped: cases.skipped };
  });
}

/** @throws None; returns a boolean for recognized sandbox launch errors. */
export function sandboxUnavailable(error: unknown): boolean {
  return /No usable sandbox|Running as root without --no-sandbox|Failed to move to new namespace|SUID sandbox|sandbox.*Operation not permitted|Failed to unshare.*Operation not permitted/i.test(String(error));
}
/** @throws E_PROOF_BROWSER for missing playwright-core; invalid deadline, browser launch failure or callback error; bounded context/browser cleanup failure. */
export async function withBrowser<T>(options: LaunchOptions & Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> & { chromium?: BrowserType }, proof: (context: BrowserContext) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const { chromium: suppliedChromium, executablePath, cwd: _cwd, logsDir: _logs, ...other } = options;
  const requireCaller = createRequire(resolve(options.cwd ?? process.cwd(), "package.json"));
  let caller = suppliedChromium;
  if (!caller) {
    for (const peer of ["playwright-core", "@playwright/test", "playwright"]) {
      let path: string;
      try { path = requireCaller.resolve(peer); }
      catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "MODULE_NOT_FOUND") continue; throw error; }
      const peerModule = await import(path);
      caller = (peerModule.chromium ?? peerModule.default?.chromium) as BrowserType;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!caller) {
    try { caller = (await import("playwright-core")).chromium; }
    catch (cause) { throw new Error("E_PROOF_BROWSER: withBrowser requires playwright-core; run npm install --save-dev playwright-core before deploying or starting proof hosts", { cause }); }
  }
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const err = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.browser.stderr.log`), "wx", 0o600);
  const diagnostic = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.witness.private.jsonl`), "wx", 0o600);

const substringLocators = new WeakMap<Locator, { locator: Locator; scoped: boolean }>();
/** Strings match exactly by default; use exact:false or a RegExp for a row with more text. Excludes editable values. */
/** @throws None at construction; locator observation can refuse ambiguous or invalid selectors. */
export function renderedText(page: Page, text: string | RegExp, options: { within?: Locator; exact?: boolean } = {}): Locator {
  const root = options.within ?? page, rendered = page.locator(":not(input):not(textarea)");
  const locator = root.getByText(text, { exact: options.exact ?? true }).and(rendered);
  if (typeof text === "string" && options.exact !== false) substringLocators.set(locator, { locator: root.getByText(text, { exact: false }).and(rendered), scoped: options.within !== undefined });

fn bind_schedule(decl: &crate::ast::ScheduleDecl, programs: &[BoundProgram]) -> Result<snapback4_core::schema::Schedule, Diagnostic> {
    use snapback4_core::ir::{ArgType, ProgramKind};
    let rewrite = format!("job {}(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):\n  for p in profiles:\n    ...", decl.target);
    let Some(target) = programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == decl.target) else {
        let candidates: Vec<_> = programs.iter().filter(|p| matches!(p.program.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action) && p.program.name.rsplit_once('.').is_some_and(|(_, name)| name == decl.target)).collect();
        if let [target] = candidates.as_slice() {
            return Err(crate::diag::unknown(&decl.site, format!("schedule {} names bare {} {}; {} declares it as {}", decl.name, target.program.kind.word(), decl.target, target.site.file, target.program.name), target.program.name.clone()).guide("jobs").mechanical());
        }
        return Err(crate::diag::unknown(&decl.site, format!("schedule {} names no job or action {}; targets outside schema.q are named <module>.<name>", decl.name, decl.target), crate::diag::name_rewrite(&decl.target, programs.iter().filter(|p| matches!(p.program.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action)).map(|p| p.program.name.as_str()), None)).guide("jobs"));
    };
    if !matches!(target.program.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", format!("schedule {} names {}, which is a {}; a schedule fires a job or action", decl.name, decl.target, target.program.kind.word()), rewrite));
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.2 — a scheduled action names a service seat, never a caller or the system.
    match (target.program.kind, decl.service.as_deref()) {
        (ProgramKind::Action, None) => return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", "a scheduled action requires a service seat", format!("schedule {} every 1m as service:billing: {}", decl.name, decl.target)).guide("actions")),
        (ProgramKind::Job, Some(_)) => return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", "a job runs as the system; its schedule cannot declare a service seat", format!("schedule {} every 1m: {}", decl.name, decl.target))),
        (_, Some(service)) if !service.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty() && name.len() <= 64 && name.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'_' || b == b'-')) => return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", "a service seat needs 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -", "as service:billing").guide("actions")),
        _ => {}
    }
    if target.program.kind == ProgramKind::Action && decl.service.as_deref().is_some_and(|service|
        snapback4_core::engine::actions::authorize(&target.program, service).is_err()) {
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", "the scheduled service seat is refused by the action's admission rule", "name a service seat the action admits, or revise its rule").guide("actions"));
    }
    for (name, kind) in &target.program.args {
        if !(matches!(name.as_str(), "periodStart" | "periodEnd") && *kind == ArgType::Time) {
            return Err(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "E_SCHEDULE", format!("schedule {} fires {}, whose argument {name} the clock cannot supply; a scheduled job or action takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing", decl.name, decl.target), if target.program.kind == ProgramKind::Action { format!("action {}(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time)", decl.target) } else { rewrite }));
        }
    }
    Ok(snapback4_core::schema::Schedule { name: decl.name.clone(), every_ms: decl.every_ms, offset_ms: decl.offset_ms, target: decl.target.clone(), service: decl.service.clone(), months: decl.months })
}

export function writeRefusal(reply: Record<string, unknown>): boolean {
  const why = reply.denied as Refusal | undefined;
  return !!why && !why.retryable && !["E_STORE", "E_STORE_RESTORE", "E_GENERATION", "E_PREDICT"].includes(why.code);
}

export class AdmissionError extends Error {
  readonly why: Refusal;
  constructor(value: string | Refusal) { super(typeof value === "string" ? value : value.message); this.why = typeof value === "string" ? admissionRefusal(value) : value; }
}

/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing url or invalid timeout; E_AUTH naming attempted credential; catalog, clock, filesystem, row JSON/table or E_FIXTURE_TIME validation failure. */
export async function loadFixtures(directory: string, options: LoadFixturesOptions): Promise<FixtureRow[]> {
  if (!options || typeof options.url !== "string" || !options.url.trim()) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: loadFixtures requires options.url (the running dev URL)");
  const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? 10_000;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs <= 0) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: loadFixtures timeoutMs must be a positive integer");

        ? `token sha256:${createHash("sha256").update(authorization).digest("hex").slice(0, 12)}` : "no credential (dev catalog)";
      throw Error(`E_AUTH: loadFixtures could not read ${path} using ${attempted}; check the declared persona or sign in again for this server`);
    }

    return body;

  const now = status.now;
  if (typeof now !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now < 0) throw Error("E_FIXTURE_TIME: dev did not report a valid clock");
  const tables = catalog.tables as Array<{ name: string; fields: Record<string, unknown> }>;
  if (!Array.isArray(tables)) throw Error("E_INPUT: host did not report table field types");
  const dir = resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", directory), rows: FixtureRow[] = [];
  for (const name of (await readdir(dir)).filter(name => name.endsWith(".jsonl") && name !== "test.cases.jsonl").sort()) {
    const table = name.slice(0, -6), definition = tables.find(item => item.name === table);
    if (!definition) throw Error(`E_INPUT: ${name}: unknown table ${table}`);
    const lines = (await readFile(join(dir, name), "utf8")).split(/\r?\n/);
    for (let n = 0; n < lines.length; n++) {
      if (!lines[n]!.trim()) continue;
      const at = `${name}:${n + 1}`;
      let row: unknown;
      try { row = JSON.parse(lines[n]!); } catch (error) { throw Error(`E_INPUT: ${at}: ${error}`); }
      if (!row || typeof row !== "object" || Array.isArray(row)) throw Error(`E_INPUT: ${at}: expected a row object`);
      const fields = row as Record<string, unknown>;
      for (const [field, kind] of Object.entries(definition.fields)) {

function timeValue(kind: unknown, value: unknown, now: number, at: string): unknown {
  if (kind && typeof kind === "object") {
    const type = kind as Record<string, unknown>;
    if ("Optional" in type) return value === null ? null : timeValue(type.Optional, value, now, at);
    const list = (type.List ?? type.ListRange) as { of?: unknown } | undefined;
    if (list && Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item, i) => timeValue(list.of, item, now, `${at}[${i}]`));
  }
  if (kind !== "Time" || typeof value !== "string" || !value.startsWith("now")) return value;
  const match = /^(?:now|now([+-])([0-9]+)(ms|s|m|h|d|w))$/.exec(value);
  if (!match) throw Error(`E_FIXTURE_TIME: ${at}: ${JSON.stringify(value)}: expected now or now±positive integer ms/s/m/h/d/w`);
  const units: Record<string, bigint> = { ms: 1n, s: 1000n, m: 60000n, h: 3600000n, d: 86400000n, w: 604800000n };
  const delta = match[2] === undefined ? 0n : BigInt(match[2]) * units[match[3]!]!;
  const resolved = BigInt(now) + (match[1] === "-" ? -delta : delta);
  if ((match[2] !== undefined && delta === 0n) || resolved < 0n || resolved > BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)) throw Error(`E_FIXTURE_TIME: ${at}: ${JSON.stringify(value)} is outside the supported duration/time range`);
  return Number(resolved);
}

        Header::from_bytes("Vary", "Origin").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Time, X-Snapback-Clock, X-Snapback-Generation, X-Snapback-Dev-Reset").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "content-type, x-snapback-persona, x-snapback-meter, authorization, range, x-snapback-device").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS").unwrap(),
    ];
    if let Ok(Some(origin)) = single_header(request, "origin") && origin_authority(origin).is_some() {
        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin).unwrap());
    }
    if latency::headers_enabled() {
        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "Server-Timing").unwrap());
    }
    headers
}

const JSON_REQUEST_CEILING: usize = 1 << 20;
// Even six-byte JSON escapes for all 256 × 128 id bytes leave room
// for a snapshot cursor, without changing the general JSON body ceiling.
const SYNC_REQUEST_CEILING: usize = 256 << 10;
const SEED_REQUEST_CEILING: usize = 16 << 20;
pub(super) const JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING: usize = 16 << 20;

/// Stop encoding before an additional unbounded String exists. Partition
/// acquisition has its own page/commit bounds; this owns the wire buffer.
struct JsonBuffer(Vec<u8>);
impl io::Write for JsonBuffer {
    fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> io::Result<usize> {
        if bytes.len() > JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING.saturating_sub(self.0.len()) {
            return Err(io::Error::other("HTTP JSON response exceeds 16 MiB"));
        }
        self.0.extend_from_slice(bytes);
        Ok(bytes.len())
    }
    fn flush(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> { Ok(()) }
}

use crate::sync_wire::{SnapshotReply, SyncEvents, StreamReply};

/// Sample at response construction, after execution and any admission wait.
pub(crate) fn clock_headers(shared: &Shared) -> [Header; 2] {
    let clock = lock(&shared.clock);
    let now = clock.now().max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(Ordering::Acquire));
    [Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Time", now.to_string()).unwrap(),
     Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Clock", clock.revision()).unwrap()]
}

// @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — advertise only on ephemeral backends; durable-only HTTP bytes stay unchanged.
// The online tier can discover /schema facts through its ordinary traffic.
pub(crate) fn ephemeral_headers(shared: &Shared) -> Vec<Header> {
    if lock(&shared.wake).eph.tables.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    vec![Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Ephemeral", "1").unwrap(),
         Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Ephemeral").unwrap()]

    /// Adds a header to the list.
    /// Does all the checks.
    pub fn add_header<H>(&mut self, header: H)
    where
        H: Into<Header>,
    {
        let header = header.into();

        // ignoring forbidden headers
        if header.field.equiv("Connection")
            || header.field.equiv("Trailer")
            || header.field.equiv("Transfer-Encoding")
            || header.field.equiv("Upgrade")
        {
            return;
        }

        // if the header is Content-Length, setting the data length
        if header.field.equiv("Content-Length") {
            if let Ok(val) = usize::from_str(header.value.as_str()) {
                self.data_length = Some(val)
            }

            return;
        // if the header is Content-Type and it's already set, overwrite it
        } else if header.field.equiv("Content-Type") {
            if let Some(content_type_header) = self
                .headers
                .iter_mut()
                .find(|h| h.field.equiv("Content-Type"))
            {
                content_type_header.value = header.value;
                return;
            }
        }

        self.headers.push(header);
    }

    /// Returns the same request, but with an additional header.
    ///
    /// Some headers cannot be modified and some other have a
    ///  special behavior. See the documentation above.
    #[inline]
    pub fn with_header<H>(mut self, header: H) -> Response<R>
    where
        H: Into<Header>,
    {
        self.add_header(header.into());
        self
    }

    /// Returns the same request, but with a different status code.
    #[inline]
    pub fn with_status_code<S>(mut self, code: S) -> Response<R>
    where
        S: Into<StatusCode>,
